HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"blocked","docket_ready":false}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_19990217ffe2faa68e30","text":"1. **Readiness is morally neutral; the destination is not.** `نُيَسِّرُهُ` again anchors `ي س ر:B001` (making ready), while `ٱلْعُسْرَىٰ` anchors `ع س ر:B001` (difficulty and severity). The exact syntax of 92:7 is retained but its destination is reversed, after withholding and denial in 92:8–9. The packet supplies symmetry; the abductive move is that repeated conduct makes a person increasingly ready for its own kind of outcome. Facilitation therefore becomes a morally neutral process of fit, capable of smoothing entry into difficulty.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001694/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_1a52d7c0cbf5e734d37a","text":"1. **Facilitation toward hardship.** Evidence: `ي س ر` B001 (making ready/easy), `ع س ر` B001 (difficulty/severity). Structural cue: same facilitation verb as 92:7 but destination inverted. Mechanism: ease here means unhindered movement into constriction. Abductive move: I infer that divine enabling can confirm a chosen trajectory without approving it. Reading change: terrifyingly, “making easy” can describe smooth descent into difficulty.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B001","root_001694/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2461e2171c8c4b7a4524","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:10:1\",\"root_001694\",[\"B001\"]],[\"92:10:2\",\"root_001012\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Kolaylığın önce kolay yola, burada ise zorluğa hazırlaması, hazırlanmanın tek başına olumlu olmadığını gösterir: tekrarlanan seçimler kişiyi zorluğa da akıcı biçimde uygun hâle getirebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_713ed78e781f93d0b075","text":"No later ayah follows, but the whole packet returns here: night/day disclosure, divergent striving, failed wealth, guidance, warning, and purified giving all resolve as رضا. The surprise is that the last word is not “reward” or “fire” but acceptance/pleasure, preserving both divine approval and the purified person’s satisfaction.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_77d661662770982d0295","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"92:10:2\",\"root_001012\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Yaratılış ve üretkenlik bağlamı, zorluğu vermemenin yol açtığı sonuçsuz bir doğum sancısı gibi göstermeye elverir; bu bağlantı çok dolaylıdır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_79af63dc66d8db57197f","text":"3. **A self-tightening course.** `ع س ر:B004` supplies opposition, entanglement, and making difficult. The repeated facilitation formula means that even complication can become habitual and easy to enter; the subsequent fall in 92:11 and warning in 92:14 give that entanglement a directional endpoint. The causal loop is inferred from sequence rather than stated. The fixed ayah changes into a mechanism of self-reinforcing complication, not a sudden externally imposed hardship.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9e8a6fc3789fe093db25","text":"2. **Hardship as insolvency of retained wealth.** `ع س ر:B002` contributes straitness of means, and `ع س ر:B003` adds the pressure of an insolvent debtor. Withholding and claimed sufficiency in 92:8 are followed by the declaration in 92:11 that wealth will not avail. The packet gives the wealth sequence; the inferred arrow is that retention culminates in a form of insolvency precisely when wealth is required to stand in. `ٱلْعُسْرَىٰ` thus reads not only as generic difficulty but as the failure of a possession-based account to settle what confronts it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d289b8e71b5aadffea11","text":"2. **Financial straitness beneath self-sufficiency.** Evidence: `ع س ر` B002 (straitness of means), B003 (pressing an insolvent debtor), activated by 92:8 wealth claim and 92:11 non-availing مال. Structural cue: self-sufficiency is followed by debt-like failure. Mechanism: withheld wealth becomes unable to discharge the person’s own liability. Abductive move: I infer a moral-debt ledger from the later recompense/favor language. Reading change: the “hardest” path includes insolvency before the final account.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B002","root_001012/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_d68d1559fa92ecace9b1","text":"92:11 unexpectedly specified hardship as failed means, activating `ع س ر:B002/B003` rather than generic severity alone. The warning and fire in 92:14–15 then gave `ع س ر:B004` an endpoint: entanglement is read as a course converging on exposure, with that causal direction explicitly inferred.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001012/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dd72b131d26910b62bf0","text":"3. **Difficult childbirth and blocked generation.** Evidence: `ع س ر` B006 (difficult childbirth), activated by 92:3 male/female and womb/mercy branches. Structural cue: generative creation stands at the beginning; hard facilitation stands after sterile withholding. Mechanism: refusal to give turns the generative order into obstructed birth. Abductive move: I infer that moral non-generativity is imaged as labor that cannot deliver.
